w000001.1 | the | det. | 这, 那, 该 | used before a noun that is known or specific | Can you close the door, please? | 请你把门关上好吗？
w000002.1 | to | prep. | 向, 往, 到 | in the direction of a place | I walk to school every morning. | 我每天早上走路去上学。
w000002.2 | to | prep. | 给, 对, 对于 | showing who receives or is affected by something | She gave the book to her brother. | 她把书给了她弟弟。
w000002.3 | to | prep. | 到, 直到 | until a stated time or point | The shop is open from nine to five. | 这家商店从九点营业到五点。
w000003.1 | and | conj. | 和, 与, 并且 | used to join words, phrases or clauses | I bought bread and milk. | 我买了面包和牛奶。
w000003.2 | and | conj. | 然后, 接着 | after that; then | He finished his homework and went to bed. | 他做完作业，然后去睡觉了。
w000004.1 | of | prep. | …的, 属于 | belonging to or connected with | The color of the car is red. | 这辆车的颜色是红色的。
w000004.2 | of | prep. | 由…组成, 含有 | containing or made up of | She drank a cup of tea. | 她喝了一杯茶。
w000004.3 | of | prep. | 关于, 有关 | about; concerning | He told us a story of his childhood. | 他给我们讲了一个关于他童年的故事。
w000005.1 | a | det. | 一, 一个, 某个 | one; any single person or thing | I saw a cat in the garden. | 我在花园里看见一只猫。
w000005.2 | a | det. | 每, 每一 | for each; per | I go swimming twice a week. | 我每周去游泳两次。
w000006.1 | in | prep. | 在…里, 在…内 | inside a place, container or area | The keys are in my bag. | 钥匙在我的包里。
w000006.2 | in | prep. | 在…期间, 在…时 | during a period of time | We usually go to the beach in summer. | 我们通常在夏天去海边。
w000006.3 | in | adv. | 进, 在里面, 在家 | into or inside a place | Come in and sit down. | 进来坐吧。
w000007.1 | for | prep. | 为, 给, 对于 | intended to be given to or used by | I made this cake for you. | 这个蛋糕是我为你做的。
w000007.2 | for | prep. | 为了, 以便 | with the purpose of | We went out for a walk. | 我们出去散步了。
w000007.3 | for | prep. | 持续, 达 | showing a length of time or distance | We waited for two hours. | 我们等了两个小时。
w000007.4 | for | conj. | 因为, 由于 | because; since | She stayed at home, for she was very tired. | 她待在家里，因为她非常累。
w000008.1 | that | det. | 那, 那个 | the one further away or already mentioned | Who is that man over there? | 那边那个男人是谁？
w000008.2 | that | pron. | 那, 那个, 那件事 | a thing or idea already mentioned | That was a really good idea. | 那真是个好主意。
w000008.3 | that | conj. | 引导从句, 以致 | used to introduce a clause stating a fact or idea | I know that you are right. | 我知道你是对的。
w000008.4 | that | adv. | 那么, 那样 | to such a degree; so | The film wasn't that bad. | 那部电影没那么糟糕。
w000009.1 | you | pron. | 你, 你们 | the person or people being spoken to | Are you ready to go? | 你准备好走了吗？
w000009.2 | you | pron. | 任何人, 人们 | people in general | You can't buy happiness with money. | 人用钱买不到幸福。
w000010.1 | it | pron. | 它, 这, 那 | the thing or animal already mentioned | I lost my pen, but I found it later. | 我丢了钢笔，不过后来找到了。
w000010.2 | it | pron. | 这事, 那事, 情况 | a situation or fact being talked about | It's very cold today. | 今天很冷。

w000011.2 | on | prep. | 在…日, 在…时 | used with days and dates | My birthday is on Monday. | 我的生日在星期一。
w000011.3 | on | prep. | 关于, 有关 | about a particular subject | She wrote a book on Chinese history. | 她写了一本关于中国历史的书。
